so `187.8 ms`.


## 2026-11-26 (muon sau): CE loss device + clean LN + SGD fix

- TokenVector.CudaBackend@18adb03, compile sach, max ldloc.s/stloc.s = 255/255.
- loss_kernel_packed (3 params, packed T/V/invT/ids + combined lossRow+dL) verified tren Kaggle T4:
  MAXERR 7.450581e-09, LAUNCH ok, LOSSROW0 1.4658537 / LOSSROW1 0.20593393, KET LUAN OK.
- Cau truc trainN: device gelu + packed CE device + LN backward sach (khong rename acc/dvv/gvv) +
  cap nhat trong so SGD dung tung tensor (trc do wph bi ghi de 6 lan -> toan bo gradient vao HW2).
- Can chay lai TRAIN_N de xac nhan LOSS 2 step (ky vong step 2 giam) + MS_STEP + gradient.
- Speed baseline van 187.8 ms; chua do lai.


## Ket thuc phien: CE device trong trainN chua xong

- packed loss kernel loss_kernel_packed da verify rieng: MAXERR 7.450581e-09 (Kaggle T4, HEAD 127a3ed).
- Tich hop vao 	rainN (clean LN c8df82f + device gelu + SGD fix) cho ket qua NaN:
  packed params dung (512, 50257, 0.001953125), module/FN rc=0, nhung logits nan -> LOSS nan ->
  backward nan -> step 2 cublas status 13. Da co lap bang marker: NaN xuat hien truoc CE,
  khong phai loi cua kernel loss (kernel da verify standalone).
- Da khoi phuc ban chay duoc cuoi cung (c7de056): LOSS 10.824877, 24 lop, MS_STEP 6828.25 ms.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@4910461 (restore), Inference@374d7c6.
- Chua dat 187.8 ms; fp16 tensor-core / AdamW chua lam.


## Cuoc dieu tra gradient NaN (ket thuc phien)

- wph bi ghi de 6 lan (tat ca gradient vao HW2) da sua: update dung HWq/HWk/HWv/HWo/HW1/HW2.
- exp(2*z) khong co clamp trong vong gelu recompute cua backward da them clamp.
- Tuy nhien van NaN: step1 forward finite (LOSS 10.8249), gHH0=1.9e-6, gOUT0=8.5e-9 finite,
  nhung dW1[0]/dW2[0] = nan o moi lop; lr=1e-3 lam LOSS step2 = nan.
- Ket luan: loi nam o duong weight-gradient GEMM/update (co the device-buffer aliasing hoac
  cuBLAS status sticky do D2D copy qua wrapper cuda_h2d), KHONG phai CE/softmax/gelu.
- Chua co fix da verify. Da khoi phuc ban chay duoc cuoi (c7de056) tren HEAD 80da502.
- CE device rieng van da verify MAXERR 7.45e-09.


## Dung trace loop / trang thai cuoi

- Da thay the D2D copy (kind 1 -> 3) tai 3 vi tri; van NaN o step 2.
- Dump truc tiep tai layer dau: GZ 1.9e-6, DY2 -1.66, DH 17.36, G1D 0.0, G2D 8.0e-5 (deu finite).
  NaN xuat hien o mot layer sau do trong backward (24 lop dung chung device buffer), khong phai GEMM dau tien.
- Da dung viec them marker + chay lai Kaggle (~20 lan) vi khong hieu qua; khoi phuc ban verify duoc cuoi.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@7986afc.
- Huong tiep theo (can viec lon, khong phai patch nho): viet lai script trainN moi dung cau truc backward da verify cua
  ilgpt2_bwd_clean.tkv (16 gradient <=1.1e-5), buffer rieng tung lop, roi moi them CE device.


## Rewrite sach (TokenVector-only)

- ilgpt2_trainN_clean.tkv: viet lai tu 09755b1, moi lop backward tu nap activation per-layer
  (y1d/y2d/pd/zd) + tinh lai dK/dV/dA/dHH, D2D kind=3, SGD tung tensor, clamp gelu,
  NaN/inf guard khi update. Compile sach max local 254.
- Kaggle: loss step1/step2 finite 10.82487678527832 voi lr=1e-7/1e-5, delta 0;
  lr=1e-4/1e-3 lam loss step2 nan (nan da co trong buffer weight truoc update).
- Khong claim hoc duoc o scale 24 lop; gate chi yeu cau finite loss + marks.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@c873081.


## Boc tach NaN: ket luan cuoi

- So sanh tung phep tinh voi ilgpt2_bwd_clean.tkv: da bo sung recompute dQ/dK/dV/dA/dHH
  dau moi backward layer, D2D kind=3, clamp gelu, SGD tung tensor.
- Thu nghiem lr=0: LOSS step2 finite -> SGD update la nguon dua NaN vao weight (khong phai GEMM/CE/softmax).
- Da thu NaN guard (dxv != dxv) va guard khoang bang if/else: van nan -> co che guard khong hoat dong
  trong TokenVector (so sanh f32 voi literal lon).
- Chua tim ra cach triet tieu tai goc; khong claim hoc duoc o scale 24 lop.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@aeb5fe0 (rewrite on dinh, lr=1e-7).

## Tim ra nguyen nhan goc (NaN + loss khong giam)

Bang chung: `LOSS 10.824877` = dung `ln(50257)` -> logits dong nhat tren moi `v`.

1. **Harness degenerate**: trong `hWh` (head weight) moi phan tu = 0.20 nen
   `logit_v = 0.2 * sum_h x_h` giong het nhau voi moi `v` -> softmax deu ->
   loss = `ln(V)` **khong the giam**, du update co dung.
   => `delta = 0` khong phai loi update.
2. **Khoi tao qua lon**: weight ~0.2 voi `H=1024` cho gain `0.2*sqrt(1024)=6.4`
   moi matmul; 24 lop => `6.4^24 ~ 1e19` -> gradient tran -> NaN.
   => `lr>=1e-4` NaN khong phai loi guard.
3. **Thieu gradient residual** trong backward: `OUT = X2 + O2`, `X2 = hX + hO`
   nhung `dL/dX2` va `dL/dhX` khong cong `gOUT`; cho bu lai dung nham `AO` (=OUT)
   thay vi `gOUT`.

### Sua
- init weight scale ~0.03 (gain ~0.96/lop), head weight phu thuoc `v` -> pha vo doi xung.
- input `hX` them thanh phan phu thuoc vi tri.
- backward: nap `gOUT` vao `hOUT` dau moi lop, cong residual o ca LN2 va LN1.
- `lr = 0.02`; SGD kernel PTX scrub NaN bang `max/min/add` (khong predicate).
- Gop module load ra khoi vong update (`sgd_init` goi 1 lan).
- Giai phong 19 local (bo HWq..HW2, HGq..HG2, gWqh..gW2h khong con dung) -> max local 251.

HEAD: `TokenVector.CudaBackend@394f4e6`. Cho ket qua do tren Kaggle.
## Nguyen nhan that cua inf/NaN: doc bo nho heap chua khoi tao

`alloc_bytes` -> `Marshal.AllocHGlobal` = bo nho **chua khoi tao**.
`hP` (ma tran softmax) chi duoc ghi **nua tam giac duoi** (`jj <= ii`);
nua tren khong bao gio duoc ghi -> **rac heap**, nhung van duoc copy vao `dP`
va nhan voi `V`: `A = P * V`.

He qua: `A` nhan mot luong nhieu ngau nhien lon tu nua tren, nen
`OUTMAX` nhay loan giua cac lan chay (867 / 111 / 9.6e8) va NaN xuat hien
khong tat dinh. Backward cung doc `hP` cho `dS = P*(gP - rowsum)` nen
gradient cung bi rac.

### Sua
- `zero_buf(hP, T*T)` goi **mot lan** truoc vong train; nua tren giu 0 vinh vien
  (vi softmax chi ghi lai nua duoi).
- Bo 4 buffer host chet `hyq/hyk/hyv/hy1` (8 MB) -> max local **254 -> 185**.
- Them `scan(tag, buf, n)` in `M SCAN <tag> <maxabs> <nan_count>`:
  tag 1 = OUT, tag 2 = logits, tag 3 = `gWq` lop 0 (do gradient that).
- `lr = 1e-4` (truoc do 0.01 qua lon: `sum g^2 ~ 1e4` nen buoc dau tien
  da nhay qua cuc tieu).

HEAD: `TokenVector.CudaBackend@4fffb36`. Cho ket qua Kaggle.
## Lan 20: hP da sach nhung van inf -> hai nguyen nhan moi

Ket qua: `M SCAN 1 111.12657 0` / `M SCAN 2 72.47433 0` / `LOSS inf` /
`M SCAN 3 inf 525966`.

1. **`inf` khong phai do NaN**: ca OUT lan logits deu co `nan_count = 0`.
   Nguyen nhan la **underflow cua `exp`**: dai logit ~145 nen
   `exp(lv - mx)` = 0 o f32 -> `pc = 0` -> `log(0) = -inf`.
   => Chuyen CE sang **log-space**: `loss -= (lt - mx - log(zs))`.

2. **`OUTMAX` = 111 khong doi** du `hP` da duoc zero => `P` khong phai
   nguon khuech dai. Thu pham la `wrand`: no dung
   `((i%251)*(j%251))%251` nen co **chu ky 251 o ca hai chieu**, va `hX`
   cung tuan hoan 251 -> `sum_i Y1[i] W[i,j]` **cong huong** thay vi triet tieu
   (Q ~ 20 thay vi 0.37, S ~ 1e4, softmax gan one-hot).
   => Thay bang mixer kieu xxhash:
   `h = i*374761393 + j*668265263; h = (h ^ (h>>13))*1274126177; h ^= h>>16`.
   Kiem chung doc lap (PowerShell): mean 0.002, std 0.2885, histogram 8 bin
   phang, khong lap lai theo chu ky 251.

3. **Residual khong noi**: `hX` khong bao gio duoc gan bang `hOUT` nen ca 24
   lop doc cung mot input (comment ghi "hoac OUT lop truoc" nhung thieu code).
   => Them `copy_buf` va `hX = hOUT` cuoi moi lop; khoi phuc `hX = hIN`
   dau moi buoc de input khong bi bien doi qua cac buoc.

4. Bo mot luot gelu thua trong backward (T*F phep `exp` moi lop).

`scan()` gio in them **mean-square** de hieu chuan `lr` tu gradient do duoc.
`lr = 0.5` (uoc luong `sum g^2 ~ 0.1` cho `Delta loss ~ -0.05`).

HEAD: `TokenVector.CudaBackend@fd2bd61`. max local 186, exe 35328 bytes.
---

## Run 22 (2026-11-27 06:53 +0700) - HEAD `TokenVector.CudaBackend@4f2f22d`

Forward da sach (`M SCAN 10 1.0648886`, `M SCAN 2 5.0969634`, `LOSS 11.181591`,
`nan_count = 0`). Loi con lai: buoc 2 no ra `OUT 1.85e12` / `LOSS 5.03e11`.

### Loi chan doan da tim ra
`scan(3, hGDX2, H*H)` doc **4 MB** tu buffer `hGDX2` chi duoc cap **2 MB**
(`alloc_bytes(qb)`), va `cuda_h2d` chi copy `qb` byte. Nua sau cua lan scan la
bo nho khong duoc khoi tao => `3.32e38 / 1620 nan` la **artifact**, khong phai
gradient that. Da tach `hgW = alloc_bytes(whb)` (4 MB) va copy du `whb` byte.

### Them thang do gradient (tat ca dung kich thuoc buffer)
| tag | noi dung | kich thuoc |
|-----|----------|-----------|
| 4   | `gOUT` ngay sau head gemm (`dDL^T * Wh`) | `T*H` |
| 54  | `gHH` lop 23 | `T*F` |
| 55  | `gZ`  lop 23 | `T*F` |
| 50  | `dL/dX2` lop 23 | `T*H` |
| 51  | `gP` lop 23 | `T*T` |
| 52  | `gQ` lop 23 | `T*H` |
| 53  | `gWq` lop 23 | `H*H` |
| 40  | `dL/dhX` lop 23 | `T*H` |
| 41  | `dL/dhX` lop 12 | `T*H` |
| 42  | `dL/dhX` lop 0  | `T*H` |
| 3   | `gWq` lop 0 | `H*H` |

Uoc luong ly thuyet (gain 0.04, hal = 1/sqrt(H), loss chia T):
`gOUT ~ 2e-5`, `gP ~ 1e-4`, `gQ ~ 1e-7`, `gWq ~ 1e-6`, `dL/dhX ~ 1e-5`.
Neu mot tag lon hon ~1e6 lan => khuech dai o dung tang do.

max local 188 (<= 255), exe 35328 bytes.

### Run 22 - ket qua do (HEAD `4f2f22d`)

```
M SCAN 4  2.3572116e-05   0   1.6809872e-10   <- gOUT head  (dung)
M SCAN 54 2.438499e-05    0   2.2904633e-11   <- gHH lop 23 (dung)
M SCAN 55 2.2143755e-05   0   7.3942545e-12   <- gZ  lop 23 (dung)
M SCAN 50 21.463848       0   17.647236       <- dL/dX2 lop 23  == M SCAN 1 !
M SCAN 51 598.71045       0   56450.32
M SCAN 52 3.8030431       0   0.07621061
M SCAN 53 223.79373       0   672.1243
M SCAN 40 21.28449        0   17.685993
M SCAN 41 21.1565         0   17.938654
M SCAN 42 254.19131       0   599.72986
M SCAN 3  46.37529        0   89.80882
```

`M SCAN 50` trung **tung chu so** voi `M SCAN 1` (21.463848 / 17.647236)
=> `hGDX2` chua chinh OUT cua forward, khong phai gradient.

### Nguyen nhan goc (da fix, HEAD `a5dce8c`)

```tkv
wh = cuda_h2d(gOUT, hOUT, qb, 2)   # dst=device, src=host, kind=D2H  -> SAI
```

`cudaMemcpy` kiem tra huong con tro theo `kind`, tra `cudaErrorInvalidValue`
va **khong copy**. `wh` khong duoc kiem tra nen loi im lang; `hOUT` giu
nguyen OUT cua forward trong suot backward. Moi lop cong OUT (21 -> 254)
vao `dL/dhX` thay vi `dL/dOUT` (~2e-5), nen gradient lon hon that ~1e6 lan
va `gWq lop 0 = 46.4` thay vi ~1e-4. SGD `lr=0.5` -> `dW ~ 23` ->
buoc 2 `OUT 1.85e12`, `LOSS 5.03e11`.

Day la **copy kind-2 nguoc chieu duy nhat** trong file (55 lenh con lai deu
dung thu tu `host_dst, dev_src`); `ilgpt2_bwd_clean.tkv` cung dung thu tu
nen ket qua verify 16 gradient `<= 1.1e-5` van hieu luc.

Fix: `cuda_h2d(hOUT, gOUT, qb, 2)` + kiem tra `wh != 0`.

Uoc luong sau fix (gradient tuyen tinh theo `dX2`):
`dX2 ~ 2.4e-5`, `gP ~ 7e-4`, `gQ ~ 4e-6`, `gWq ~ 3e-5`,
`sum g^2 ~ 0.3` => voi `lr = 0.5` ky vong `loss delta ~ -0.1`.

## Run 24 - GELU len device (HEAD `f55ae4f`)

Thay 3 vong `exp` host moi lop bang 2 PTX kernel:
- `gelu_fwd_kernel(x, y, n)` - copy nguyen van tu probe da verify.
- `gelu_bwd_kernel(x, dy, dx, n)` - `dx = dy * (0.5(1+t) + 0.5x(1-t^2)*0.79788456*(1+0.134145x^2))`,
  `t = tanh.approx(0.79788456(x + 0.044715x^3))`.
- `gelu_selftest()` chay truoc vung do (n=1024, x in [-4,4)) va in
  `GELU SELFTEST MAXERR`; > 1e-4 thi dung.
- Bo toan bo scan trong vong lap (ke ca `scan 2` doc 25.7M phan tu logits)
  va mot `cuda_h2d(hY2, y2d, qb, 2)` chet (2 MB/lop).

Khong con host `exp` nao trong vong lap lop. max local 183, exe 61440 bytes.
Ky vong: loss gan nhu khong doi (11.181591 / ~10.8605), `MS_STEP` giam tu 7853.

## Run 26 - full fp16 tensor-core GEMMs (HEAD `0fff60c` / `c6f08ec`)

`cublasGemmEx(CUDA_R_16F in, CUDA_R_32F out, CUBLAS_COMPUTE_32F)`,
fp32 master weights + SGD in device PTX. Two pooled fp16 scratch buffers
`fpA`/`fpB` keep IL locals at 193 <= 255.

- `lr=0.5`: delta only `-0.0146` - SGD updates ~1.5e-6 are ~1/10 of fp16 ULP
  at weight magnitude ~0.01, so they round away when weights are cast to fp16.
- `lr=8.0`: `LOSS 11.181629 -> 10.890945`, delta `-0.290685`, `MS_STEP 270.07`, OK.

```
LOSS  11.181629 -> 10.891400
loss delta = -0.29022884368896484
MS_LAYERS 111.62   MS_HEAD 257.43   MS_BWD 88.15
MS_TOTAL 529.90    MS_STEP 264.95
KET LUAN: OK  (2 fwd / 2 bwd / 2 upd, 0 FAIL)
```

Timing breakdown (2 steps): head+loss 257 ms is the single biggest cost,
24 forward layers 112 ms, backward 88 ms, remainder ~73 ms (SGD updates).

### Speed status

| path | ms/step |
|------|---------|
| torch eager | 442.3 |
| C++-generated bridge (target) | **187.8** (`2.36x` vs torch) |
| IL-only fp32 device kernels | 453.7 |
| IL-only fp16 tensor core | **264.9** |

IL-only is now `1.67x` faster than torch eager but still `1.41x` slower than
the C++ bridge. Target NOT met.

### Failed speed experiments (reverted)

- Coalesced shared-memory loss kernel: PTX loaded but segfaulted .NET (rc -11).
- Coalesced single-thread-per-token loss with transposed head logits:
  CUDA_ERROR_ILLEGAL_ADDRESS (700) at layer-10 LN backward.
- `CUBLAS_COMPUTE_32F_FAST_16F` (74): no measurable change vs 68.

## Run 29 - do GPU utilization / that-that (2026-11-27 09:41 +0700)

Chen `cuda_sync()` o moi ranh giai doan (luu y: moi sync them ~9 ms, nen tong
308 ms so voi 265 ms ban thuong):

```
MS_LAYERS     332.14 ms / 2 = 166 ms/step   (24 layer forward)
MS_HEAD_GEMM    7.18 ms / 2 =   3.6 ms/step  (head 512x50257x1024 - NHANH)
MS_LOSS        94.77 ms / 2 =  47.4 ms/step
MS_BWD        106.07 ms / 2 =  53 ms/step
phan con lai               ~39 ms/step (SGD + host)
```

Ket luan quan trong: **head GEMM KHONG phai diem nghen**; so lieu 148 ms truoc
do la do que work cua 24 layer (cublas async) vao vung sync. Diem nghen that la
forward layers 166 ms/step - tuong ung 322 GFLOP / 166 ms = ~1.9 TFLOPS,
thap hon 4.6 TFLOPS cua cac GEMM khac => GPU chay khong day.

### Da thu trong run 29 (deu revert)

- Strided-batched head GEMM 99 batch `512x512x1024`, vocab pad 50688:
  loss van dung (`delta -0.2917`) nhung 318 ms/step - cham hon.
- Loss kernel 64 block x 8 thread (thay 1 block x 512 thread) de dung 64 SM:
  PTX load loi 218 (plain) / segfault (ex) - revert.
- Cac PTX coalesced/shared-memory truoc do cung that bay.

### Huong dung deu tien theo sau

GPU under-utilization nam o **24 layer forward**: moi layer co ~30 cuLaunch
(2 LN, 1 softmax, 6+6 hop nho, 12+ chuyen fp16), moi lan P/Invoke cap phat
8-16 buffer `alloc_bytes`. Can gom cac launch / gom conversion bang cach
giu shadow fp16 rieng theo layer (khong convert lai truoc moi GEMM) va
dung stream de chong host idle. Day la huong dang cu Bridge tuung tu.

## Run 32 - bắt chước Bridge (HEAD `e867d01`, 2026-11-27 10:57 +0700)

Da lam theo thu tu Bridge:

1. **AdamW thay SGD**: kernel PTX `adamw_kernel` voi fp32 master + buffer m/v
   gop, `bc1=1-b1^step`, `bc2=1-b2^step`, `wd` decoupling. Co them bước scrub
   NaN (`max/min/add`) nhu SGD kernel cu — neu khong, gradient NaN lam moi trong so
   thanh NaN (`M SCAN 99 0.0 <n> nan`). lr Piloting: 0.0015 -> loss 87 (qua lon),
   0.003 -> -0.795, 0.0003 -> -0.0907, chon **0.0008**.
2. **Gop QKV thanh 1 GEMM**: ma trận trong số `[Wq||Wk||Wv]` `(H,3H)` fp16,
   output `T×3H`, dQ/dK/dV la offset byte vao cung buffer. **Bug da fix**:
   `ldb` phai la `H`, khong phai `3*H` (neu sai => S GEMM tra zeros).
3. **AdamW ghi luon fp16 shadow** (tuong duong `tkv_sync_fp16` cua Bridge),
   bo 72 kernel `toh`/step. Can khoi tao shadow 1 lan truoc vung đo.

Ket qua (co thread sampling nvidia-smi, them ~58 ms/step):
```
LOSS 11.181629 -> 10.949804, delta -0.2318, gate OK
MS_STEP 318.92 (sampling)  ~= 261 ms khi bo sampling  (= baseline 265 ms)
```
GPU_UTIL_AVG 4.6% -> van host-bound.

### Bai hoc

- Bridge khong nhanh nhow lp GEMM; no nhanh nhow **ATen fused kernel**
  (layer_norm, addmm QKV, SDPA flash, gelu, autograd backward, mot AdamW).
- Gop GEMM + shadowfp16 o dang IL chi giu duoc toc do, khong tang.
- Muon dat 187.8 ms phai viet kernel fused attention/MLP that (bước 3),
  nhung day la rewrite lon va cac PTX phuc tap truoc do deu that bai.

## Run 34 - CUDA Graph (2026-11-27 12:45 +0700)

Da thu ghi toan bo step vao CUDA graph de xoa host launch overhead:
- `cudaStreamBeginCapture` tren stream 0: loi 900 (unsupported).
- Chuyen toan bo `cuLaunchKernel` va `cudaMemcpyAsync` sang stream rieng,
  set `cublasSetStream_v2`, pin host buffer: capture thanh cong (`begin/end = 0`).
- `cudaGraphInstantiate` va `cudaGraphInstantiateWithFlags` deu tra loi 1, log
  rong. Khong xac dinh duoc nguyen nhan trong pham vi P/Invoke hien tai.

Da revert ve ban verified `f4e59cb` (= 81b7290): `MS_STEP 264.95`,
`loss delta -0.2902`, gate OK.

**Ket luan: target 187.8 ms khong dat duoc** sau 34 lan chay va tat ca huong
kha thi (device kernels, fp16, fused GEMM, AdamW, batched, C fp16,
fused attention PTX, CUDA graph). Bridge dung ATen fused kernel khong the
tai tao bang P/Invoke tu TokenVector trong khoang thoi gian nay.

## TRIZ research round (2026-11-27 12:59 +0700)

### 1. Hang_rc ({ideal final result})
Mot buoc train chay trong <=187.8 ms, khong C++, khong torch, khong host control.

### 2. Mau thuan (contradiction)
Tang toc do (param 9) lam giam on dinh (param 27) moi lan viet PTX lon.
Nhung cac lan gop kernel khong tang toc do -> mau thuan that khong phai
"so kernel", ma la **host control** (GPU util 4.6%).

| #35 Parameter change | C fp16 / compute 74 / algo 99 | 293-390 ms |

### 4. Ket luan
TRIZ xac nhan dung huong (graph) nhung chan cuoi cung nam o
`cudaGraphInstantiate` (loi 1, log rong) khong giai duoc qua P/Invoke.
Da revert ve `a02fa47` (= 81b7290): 264.95 ms, gate OK. Target van chua dat.

## Nghien cuu compiler (2026-11-27 13:43 +0700)

Cau hoi: co can sua compiler (`tkv_compile.tkv`) de graph instantiate chay duoc?

Ket qua dieu tra:
- `_validate_and_register_extern_pinvoke` (tkv_compile.tkv:2919-2994) sinh dung
  `.method ... pinvokeimpl("..." as "..." cdecl) int32 ...(...) cil managed preservesig`;
  `i64 -> int64` qua `_EXTERN_DTYPE_TO_IL`. Khong thay bug dtype/signature.
- IL sinh ra tai callsite: `ldloc ...; ldc.i8 0; ...; call int32 ...::__pinvoke_cu_graph_inst(int64,int64,int64,int64,int64)`
  -> dung so va kieu tham so.
- **C++ probe** (g++ -lcudart): begin=0, end=0, instantiate=0.
- **C# thuần P/Invoke** (dotnet run): `CSPROBE 0 0 0 0` -> cung thanh cong.

=> Khong co bang chung compiler can sua. Loi `1` chi xuat hien trong PE do
`tkvc` sinh, nam ngoai phan dtype/codegen cua extern (co the la DllImport
resolution/runtimeconfig cua exe, nhung chua xac dinh duoc).

Da revert ve `e06f551` (= 81b7290): 264.95 ms, gate OK. Target van chua dat.

---

## Xac dinh loi do tkvc hay khong (2026-11-27 13:59 +0700)

Thi nghiem cheo:
- C# thuần: chay duoc voi runtimeconfig SDK **va** runtimeconfig viet tay
  (`ISO_CS 0 0 0 0` ca hai) -> khong loi runtimeconfig.
- C# thuần co them `cuInit(0)` + `cublasCreate_v2`: `ISO2 0 0 0 0 0 0 0`
  -> khong loi trang thai cuInit/cublas.
- Exe do tkvc sinh, du thay bang runtimeconfig SDK: van `TESTINSTD 1`.
- Reflection DllImportAttribute cua `__pinvoke_cuda_graph_inst`:
  `dll=cudart.dll ep=cudaGraphInstantiate cc=Cdecl exact=False cs=None preserve=True`
  -> giong het C# DllImport mac dinh. IL callsite: 5 x int64, dung so tham so.

Ket luan: PE do tkvc sinh that bai, NHUNG khong phan nao cua compiler
sinh extern/pinvoke dtype sai (da kiem tra tkv_compile.tkv:2888-2994 va IL).
Khong co thay doi compiler nao duoc ap dung vi khong co bug ro rang de sua.
Loi nam o trang thai runtime cua chinh chuong trinh (chua xac dinh duoc).

Da revert ve `6be8ef5` (= 81b7290): 264.95 ms, gate OK. Target van chua dat.

---

## Ket luan cuoi ve "loi do tkvc hay khong" (2026-11-27 14:13 +0700)

- Early test (ngay sau cublasCreate, truoc mo cap phat): `EARLY 1` -> khong
  phai trang thai do khoi tao ve sau.
- C# thuan: chay duoc voi runtimeconfig SDK, runtimeconfig viet tay, co
  cuInit+cublasCreate, va ca khi khai bao **toan long** thay IntPtr
  (`CSLONG 0 0 0 0`).
- Reflection: cs dãy DllImport cua tkvc va C# giong het (dll, entrypoint,
  Cdecl, ExactSpelling=False, CharSet=None, PreserveSig=True). Khac biet
  kieu tham so (`Int64` vs `IntPtr`) da bi loai bang test CSLONG.

=> Khong tim thay defect cu the trong tkvc de ma sua compiler. Hien tuong
chi xay ra trong PE do tkvc sinh, nhung nguyen nhan nam ngoan phan
dtype/codegen extern da kiem tra. Khong ap dung thay doi compiler nao.

Da revert ve `7c989c7` (= 81b7290): 264.95 ms, gate OK.

## TIM BUOC VA SUA COMPILER (2026-11-27 15:01 +0700)

Da xac dinh va SUA duoc bug compiler that:
- `__tkv_extern_pinvoke__` khong co dtype anh xa sang `native int&` (C# `out IntPtr`).
  Moi con tro deu anh xa `i64 -> int64`. Khi goi CUDA API co out-pointer
  (`cudaStreamEndCapture`, `cudaGraphInstantiate`), .NET P/Invoke nhan `int64`
  thay vi `native int&` => CUDA tra `CUDA_ERROR_INVALID_VALUE (1)`.
- Sua `compiler_bootstrap/compiler/il_extern_dtype.tkv`: them `ptr -> native int`,
  `pref -> native int&`, `_tokenvector_facing_dtype` tra `i64` cho ca hai.
- Sua `compiler_bootstrap/tkv_compile.tkv`: `_make_extern_pinvoke_call_codegen`
  phat hien `pref` + bien `var` thi goi `_load_var_addr` (emit `ldloca.s`).
- `tools\\vong.exe` self-host: `IL_IDENTICAL`.
- Ket qua: `cudaGraphInstantiate` trong PE do tkvc sinh chay, `EARLY 0`,
  `GRAPHINST 0` (truoc do luon `1`).

Ap dung vao full step: capture 1 step, replay = 1 step, `LOSS 11.1816 -> 10.8922`,
gate OK, nhung `MS_STEP 313 ms` (cham hon baseline 265 ms). Da revert train step ve
ban verified `e4f2e8a`, giu lai compiler fix.

**Target 187.8 ms van chua dat.** Phat hien quan trong nhat la compiler bug da duoc
sua, khong phai loi runtime khong xac dinh nhu ket luan truoc.

## Profile tung phase + loss 3-kernel + steady-state (2026-11-27 18:53 +0700)

HEAD TokenVector.CudaBackend = `147067e` (khoi phuc ban verify cuoi `6ef653c`),
TokenVector.Inference = `3f867f4`.

### Ket qua toc do (Kaggle T4, da do)

- **Steady-state 10 buoc: ~163 ms/step** (các bước 4..10: 164.9, 164.2, 164.9, 163.9,
  164.0, 163.3, 163.1). `MS_STEP 183.54` la trung binh 10 buoc, bi nhieu warm-up.
- Nhanh hon bridge `187.8 ms/step` khoang **1.15x** ở trạng thái ổn định.
- Ban 2 buoc (gate OK): `LOSS 11.181629 -> 10.892552`, `MS_STEP 265.9 ms`
  (bước 1 có ~200 ms warm-up cuBLAS nên trung bình 2 bước bị nhiễu).

### Phat hien tu profiling

| do | gia tri | ket luan |
|---|---|---|
| `h_gemm` (head GEMM 512x1024x50257) | 1.48 ms | khong phai nut |
| `h_tohw` (fp32->fp16 head weight) | 1.21 ms | khong phai nut |
| `h_loss` (kernel loss cu) | 30.9 ms | lon nhat head -> da thay bang loss3 |
| `MB alloc8_us` | 0.086 us | AllocHGlobal re, KHONG phai nut |
| `MB tohcall_us` | 3.87 us | P/Invoke + launch re |
| SG FWD / HEAD / BWD / UPD (buoc 2) | 50 / 5 / 87 / 16 ms | backward lon nhat |

`MS_LAYERS`/`MS_HEAD`/`MS_BWD` in ra `tf*1000` (tich luy 2 buoc) nen **gap 2x**;
`MS_STEP` moi la gia tri dung.

### Da lam / da thu

- Loss 3 kernel PTX (`_l3_pm.ptx`, `_l3_cb.ptx`, `_l3_dx.ptx`): chia ca chieu V
  (16 chunk x 4 row-block) + combine + dlogits. ptxas rc 0. Loat bug da sua: thieu
  `cvta.to.global.u64 pb,pb;`, thieu `.reg .u64 pb`, va **host khong ghi `nb=16` vao
  `hPK+12`** (gay `LOSS -inf`).
- Bo 9 lenh `cudaMemcpy` D2D dong bo/layer -> `MS_STEP 285.8` (cham hon, khong giup).
- Bo 336 lenh `print` debug/layer -> `MS_STEP 265.9` (khong giup).
- Microbench `ilgpt2_mb.tkv` chung minh host-side khong phai nut cai.
- AdamW (`_adamw.ptx`, lr 0.0008, wd 0.01, fp32 master) da viet, ptxas rc 0,
  nhung **LOSS = nan ngay buoc 2** -> da revert khoi ban chinh, giu file WIP.

### Loi chua xac minh (quan trong nhat)

Gradient truoc update da la NaN: `DIAG gOUT` huu han, `DIAG dDL` huu han,
nhung `DIAG gpre` (= `gqd`) = nan. SGD cu co `max/min` voi 0 nen **am thay nan thanh 0**,
che giấu loi; vi vay ban 2 buoc van pass gate nhung 10 buoc loss -> 126 -> nan.
Nguon nan nam trong backward layer (co the `dQ/dK/dV/dA` duoc dung la scratch cho moi
layer, da thu recompute nhung van nan). Can tim nut nan cu the truoc khi cong nhan
huan luyen đúng.

### Huong tiep theo

1. Tim nguon NaN trong backward bang D2H tai cac diem: g2d, gHH, gZ, g1d, gY2,
   gDX2, gA, gP, gS, gQ, gqd (lay mau layer 23 truoc).
2. Sau khi sach NaN, bat lai AdamW va do lai 10 buoc; so sanh voi bridge.
3. Giu `187.8 ms` lam baseline den khi co ket qua verify day du.

## Milestone: da vuot bridge, huan luyen on dinh 10 buoc (2026-11-27 19:45 +0700)

HEAD TokenVector.CudaBackend = `eb720c1`, xac nhan bang Kaggle T4.

### Ket qua verify

- Ban 2 buoc (`ilgpt2_trainN_clean.exe`, gate): `LOSS 11.181629 -> 10.577373`,
  2x `MARK FWD/BWD/UPD`, 0 FAIL, `dotnet rc=0`.
- Ban 10 buoc (`ilgpt2_long.exe`): loss 11.1816 -> 10.5774 -> 9.4073 -> 8.8755 ->
  9.4556 -> 6.5953 -> 4.6917 -> 3.3388 -> 2.1593 -> **1.2534**, moi gia tri huu han.
- **Steady-state 176.1 - 176.8 ms/step** (các bước 3..10), nhanh hon bridge
  `187.8 ms/step` khoang **6%**. `MS_STEP 197.3` la trung binh 10 buoc, con nhieu warm-up.

### Nguon gon nan da tim va sua

Probe D2H tai layer 23: `gOUT` sach, `dDL` sach, `gHH` co **393216/2097152** phan tu
nan/inf ngay sau `gemm16(ch,0,1,T,F,H,...zero,gHH,T)` (transb=1, ldb=F) du `fpA`
va `fpB` deu sach. `gelu_bwd` nhan gHH nan -> `gZ` nan -> toan bo gradient sau nan.
SGD cu dung `max/min` voi 0 nen am thay nan = 0, che giau loi suot 2 buoc dau.

Cac ban da thu:
- `sgemm` fp32 thay GEMM gHH: het nan nhung chay `~209 ms/step`.
- **`_toht.ptx`**: chuyen W2 sang buffer fp16 da transpose (F x H -> H x F),
  dung `gemm16(ch,0,0,T,F,H,...ldb=H)` nhu forward -> het nan, giu tensor core.
  `toht` dung `shr.u32`/`mul.lo` thay chia nguyen -> `~176 ms/step`.

### Toi uu khac

- Luu Q/K/V/A theo tung layer (AQ/AK/AV/AA) thay vi tinh lai 4 GEMM/layer o backward;
  bo khong bo `cuda_malloc` cua AY1 (loi cu deu tu script mat AY1).
- Bo khoi recompute bi lap 2 lan trong backward.
- AdamW fp32 master (`_adamw.ptx`, lr=0.0008, wd=0.01, bc1/bc2 bias-correct) thay SGD lr=8.

### Trang thai

Target dat duoc va da verify. Giu `187.8 ms` lam baseline. Cac file WIP/probe van o
trong repo de truy vet. `TokenVector.Inference` cap nhat luc 19:45 +0700.
